## doc_e275032faa5e8762:page6_DFT_proposed_stereocontrol_pathways

类型：mechanism；状态：needs_review；完整性：partial

- unspecified_agent: computed Pd/L3 pathway via IM-(anti)-4ab; SMILES候选=未确认
- unspecified_agent: computed Pd/L2 pathways via IM-(anti)-4c and IM-(syn)-4h; SMILES候选=未确认
- reactant: copper-chelated nucleophile Nu; SMILES候选=未确认
- product: (E)-6b; SMILES候选=未确认
- product: (Z)-6b; SMILES候选=未确认

备注：The Pd/L3 DFT profile assigns E-selectivity to migratory insertion and reports a 10.48 kcal/mol difference between the favored anti and competing syn insertion transition states.

备注：The Pd/L2 DFT profile proposes iodide-facilitated η3–η1–η3 interconversion and a 1.51 kcal/mol lower barrier for the Z-forming attack. Computed intermediates/transition states are not isolated experiments.

备注：Figure 5C and 5E display the computed Pd/L3 and Pd/L2 free-energy pathways. The intermediates, transition states, η3–η1–η3 interconversion, and plotted energies are mechanistic calculations rather than isolated reaction experiments.
